HP_MAP_Prod · L12M report · approved version, syntax checked, not yet run on SQL Server

_report.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M: two procedures made into one

Before: _report.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M called _report.SP_BUILD_VIOLATIONS_L12M_STAGE. After: one procedure, written in the style of _old.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M. The report rules are the same. One report value is fixed: the account name Target Corporation. This page lists every change and shows the code before and after, side by side.

2 → 1stored procedures
3,241 → 2,420lines of code
43changes in the list
83code blocks compared
1report value changed (the Target account name)
List of changesKept on purposeSeen, not changedHow it was checkedCode side by side

List of changes

Each change has a number. The same numbers are shown on the code blocks below. "See in code" shows only the blocks with that change.

A. One procedure instead of two

1

The builder SP is now inside the main SP

WhatAll code of _report.SP_BUILD_VIOLATIONS_L12M_STAGE is now inside _report.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M. The line EXEC [_report].[SP_BUILD_VIOLATIONS_L12M_STAGE], the second header and the second parameter list are gone.

WhyYou asked for one SP. Now the whole report can be read from top to bottom in one place.

See in code (2 blocks)

2

The temp table #MAP_L12M_Ready is removed

WhatThe main SP created #MAP_L12M_Ready, the builder SP filled it, and then the main SP copied it into _report.Violations_L12M. Now the final INSERT reads directly from #FinalOutput and #CA_FinalOutput.

WhyThe table was only a bridge to pass rows from one SP to the other. With one SP it is not needed. The rows and the 85 columns are the same. One full copy of the data is saved, so this step is a little faster.

See in code (2 blocks)

3

The report dates are calculated one time

What@BusinessToday, @ThroughDate, @StartDate and @ExportStartDate were calculated in both SPs. Now they are calculated one time at the top. The formulas are not changed.

WhyThe same formula in two places is easy to change in only one place by mistake.

See in code (1 block)

4

One date check instead of four

WhatThere was one date check in the main SP and three more in the builder SP. I kept the one from the main SP. It already has every condition of the other three. Its error number (51000) and its message are not changed.

WhyI did not remove this check completely, because it protects the data. With a wrong start date the SP could delete report rows and not load them again.

See in code (1 block)

5

The SET options are written one time

WhatSET NOCOUNT ON, SET XACT_ABORT ON and SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL READ COMMITTED were in both SPs. They are kept one time.

WhySame settings, no need to repeat them.

See in code (1 block)

6

ALTER PROCEDURE and a new header line

WhatCREATE PROCEDURE is now ALTER PROCEDURE. One line is added at the end of the header: 11/17/26 Plamen Belezhkov: .... All old header lines are the same as before.

WhyThe SP already exists in the database, so the script must change it, not create it. CLAUDE.md rule 8 asks for the author name Plamen Belezhkov on new header lines and asks to keep the old lines.

See in code (2 blocks)

B. Removed code that is not business logic

7

Application locks are removed

WhatRemoved: @Lock, @USLock, @CALock, @ReportLock, three sp_getapplock calls, six sp_releaseapplock calls, and in the builder SP the APPLOCK_MODE check with @CAWriterMode and sp_executesql.

WhyYou asked for this. The locks do not calculate anything in the report. Good to know: before, a second run at the same time was stopped by the lock. Now the SP itself does not stop it.

See in code (1 block)

8

The @@TRANCOUNT check is removed

WhatBoth SPs started with IF @@TRANCOUNT<>0 THROW .... Both lines are removed.

WhyIt only checked how the SP was called. It is not a report rule.

See in code (1 block)

9

The check of the 85 column names is removed

WhatThe main SP read sys.columns and compared the names and the order of all 85 columns of _report.Violations_L12M with a fixed list.

WhyThe INSERT names every column, so the order of the columns in the table is not important. If a column is missing, SQL Server gives its own clear error.

See in code (1 block)

10

The checks on the staging table are removed

WhatThe builder SP checked that #MAP_L12M_Ready exists and that it is empty.

WhyThere is no staging table now (change 2).

See in code (1 block)

11

TRY / CATCH around the delete and insert is removed

WhatRemoved BEGIN TRY ... END TRY BEGIN CATCH ... END CATCH. BEGIN TRANSACTION, COMMIT TRANSACTION and SET XACT_ABORT ON stay.

WhyThe CATCH block was there to release the locks and to roll back. There are no locks now, and XACT_ABORT ON already rolls back the transaction when an error happens. So after an error the old rows are still in the table, same as before.

See in code (2 blocks)

12

The row count check after the insert is removed

WhatRemoved @ExpectedRows and the check L12M published row count does not match staging.

WhyIt counted the rows that were just inserted and compared them with the rows that were read for the insert. These two numbers are always the same.

See in code (2 blocks)

13

Three checks at the end of the builder SP are removed

WhatRemoved: (b) no other country and no other date, (c) the four period dates must be empty before 11/01/2026, (d) the row counts must match. Check (a), both countries must have rows, is kept. See change 37.

WhyChecks b, c and d test again what the INSERT before them already does with its WHERE and CASE. They cannot fail.

See in code (1 block)

14

Progress messages are removed

WhatRemoved 66 lines like RAISERROR(N'L12M stage starting #Weeks',10,1) WITH NOWAIT. In the two daily loops, removed the row counters @MAP_L12M_US_Total / @MAP_L12M_CA_Total and their monthly message.

WhyThey only print text in the Messages tab. The report does not use them. The old SP has no such messages.

See in code (63 blocks)

15

The email step is short again

WhatRemoved: @L12M_MailReturn, @L12M_MailItemID, the check of the return value and three information messages. The profile, the three recipients, the subject and the body are not changed. The email is still sent after the commit. The TRY / CATCH is kept. See change 38.

WhySimpler code. The mail call is the same as in the old SP.

See in code (1 block)

16

Old code in a comment is removed

WhatRemoved the comment -- (SELECT d.Week_Begin_Date_Alt FROM _final.Dim_Date d WITH (NOLOCK) ... GETDATE()))) after THEN @StartDate.

WhyIt is old code that says the same as @StartDate. It only makes the line long.

See in code (1 block)

C. Work that was done two times

17

Five temp tables are built one time, not two times

What#CA_Weeks, #CA_Fiscal_Week, #CA_DateRequested, #CA_PartnerLookup and #CA_SellerEmailUnion are removed. The Canada steps now read #Weeks, #Fiscal_Week, #DateRequested, #PartnerLookup and #SellerEmailUnion.

WhyEach pair had exactly the same query on the same HP_MAP_Prod / HP_QPP_Prod table, so the rows are the same. Five queries less, so the SP is a little faster, not slower.

See in code (8 blocks)

18

An alias with the same name as the column is removed

Whatdp.Product_Description AS Product_Description is now dp.Product_Description (four places).

WhyThe alias did nothing.

See in code (4 blocks)

D. Rules from CLAUDE.md

19

Rule 2: the N before string literals is removed

WhatRemoved the N prefix from 275 string literals in the code that stays, for example N'Canada' is now 'Canada'.

WhyAll these texts are plain English letters and numbers, so Unicode is not needed. The US part already had most literals without N. The result of every comparison is the same.

See in code (27 blocks)

20

Rule 7: COUNT_BIG is now COUNT

WhatCOUNT_BIG(*) AS Partner_Count and COUNT_BIG(DISTINCT output_base.SKU) are now COUNT (US and Canada, four places). The other COUNT_BIG calls were inside the removed checks.

WhyThe numbers are small. Partner_Count is only compared with 0, and the SKU count is only used for the order of the seller rank.

See in code (4 blocks)

21

Rule 4: spaces around operators and after commas

WhatFor example @P_Through_Date DATE=NULL is now @P_Through_Date DATE = NULL, and dp.Valid_From<DATEADD(DAY,1,@ThroughDate) is now dp.Valid_From < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ThroughDate).

WhyCLAUDE.md asks for readable spaces. Only spaces changed.

See in code (1 block)

22

Rule 8: comments in short and simple English

WhatThe long /* ... */ comments and the tag names such as MAP_L12M_STAGE_BEFORE_TARGET_LOCKS_V1 are replaced by short -- comments. The old comments with a date and initials (LU, CH, ED, RV, DP) are kept word for word.

WhyCLAUDE.md asks for short comments that are easy to read for a non-native English speaker, and asks to keep the history.

See in code (47 blocks)

23

Rule 3: no new database objects

WhatNothing new is created. One object is not used any more after this change: _report.SP_BUILD_VIOLATIONS_L12M_STAGE. I did not add a DROP PROCEDURE for it.

WhyDropping it is a database change, so it needs your approval first.

24

Rule 6: nothing is committed or applied

WhatThe first version was only a proposal in a new file. After your approval, the two procedures in DB_Objects.txt were replaced with the new one and the files were committed. Each later change was shown to you before it was committed. Nothing was run on a database.

WhyCLAUDE.md asks to show the full result and the differences first, and to wait for your approval.

E. Style and syntax like _old.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M

These changes are only about how the code is written. They are in almost every block, so the blocks below show them as one label: "Style".

25

No indent for the procedure body, tabs for indent

WhatStatements start at the left side. Inner lines use tabs. Before, the whole body was moved 4 spaces to the right.

WhyThis is how the old SP is written.

26

No semicolon at the end of statements

WhatNo statement ends with ;. The only ; left in the SP are inside text, for example the list of email recipients. See change 39.

WhyThe old SP has no ; on most statements. CLAUDE.md rule 9 says not to add ; unless it is necessary, and here none is necessary.

27

Commas at the start of the line

WhatColumn lists are written as ,column on a new line.

WhySame as the old SP.

28

Aliases without AS

Whatv.Fact_ID AS [Fact ID] is now v.Fact_ID [Fact ID], and FROM #Weeks AS w is now FROM #Weeks w. AS stays where T-SQL needs it (CAST(... AS ...), IDENTITY(...) AS).

WhyThe old SP writes most aliases this way. The alias names are not changed.

29

INNER JOIN is now JOIN

WhatINNER JOIN is written as JOIN. INNER LOOP JOIN is not changed, because the word INNER is needed there.

WhySame as the old SP. Both words mean the same join.

30

INSERT is now INSERT INTO

WhatINSERT #Product_Category (...) is now INSERT INTO #Product_Category (...).

WhySame as the old SP. Both forms do the same.

31

WITH ... AS (CTE) is now a subquery in FROM

WhatThe seven WITH name AS (...) queries (RankedContent, FiscalWeeks, RankedSellers, RankedNotifications, RankedLatestStrikes, ContactCandidates, SellerWeeks) are now written as FROM ( ... ) name.

WhyThe old SP uses no CTE. The inner query and the outer query are the same text, only the place of the inner query changed. The result is the same.

32

Short names for objects in HP_MAP_Prod

WhatHP_MAP_Prod._final.Dim_Date is now _final.Dim_Date, [_final].[ViolationsMAP] is now _final.ViolationsMAP, Square brackets are removed where the name has no space. Names in HP_MAP_Canada, HP_QPP_Prod and msdb still have the database name. HP_MAP_Prod.dbo.FN_GET_SELLER_STATUS keeps its full name (see change 41).

WhySame as the old SP. The SP is in HP_MAP_Prod, so both names point to the same object.

33

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS is directly above SELECT ... INTO

WhatThe DROP TABLE IF EXISTS line of #ViolationRows, #CA_ViolationRows, #UPC_US and #CA_UPC_US was some lines above its SELECT ... INTO. It is moved directly above it.

WhySame as the old SP. It is easier to see which table a block builds.

34

Short blocks on one line, very long lines on more lines

WhatCREATE INDEX, CONVERT(...) and OVER(...) are written on one line. Lines that were 500 to 2,100 characters long (column lists) are split. The column list of the final INSERT has one column per line.

WhySame as the old SP, and the long lines were not possible to read.

F. Changes after your review

35

Four unused columns are removed

What[Product Category], [Product Subcategory Enforcement], Seller_Status and [Termination Date] are removed from #MAP_L12M_US_Base, #ViolationRows and the same Canada tables. The four qualification columns are removed from #MAP_L12M_US_Seller / #MAP_L12M_CA_Seller, because only Seller_Status used them.

WhyYou asked for this. No later step reads these columns. The report takes its category, seller status and termination date from other tables, so the report is the same. All joins and filters stay, so the rows are the same. One FN_GET_SELLER_STATUS call per violation row is saved, so the loop should be a little faster.

See in code (8 blocks)

36

'Target Corporatio' is now 'Target Corporation'

WhatThe US account name for Target is fixed.

WhyYou asked for this. Good to know: this is the only change in a report value. Rows that are loaded from now on show the new name. Older rows in the table keep the cut name until a full refresh. If Tableau has a filter on the old text, it needs the same fix.

See in code (1 block)

37

The check that both countries have rows is back

WhatBefore the delete, the SP stops with error 51000 if #FinalOutput or #CA_FinalOutput has no rows for the export dates. The error text is the same as before.

WhyYou asked to put this QA back. If one country has no data, the old rows stay in the report table.

See in code (1 block)

38

A Database Mail error cannot fail the SP

WhatThe email step is inside BEGIN TRY ... END TRY. If the mail call fails, the CATCH block only prints a message.

WhyYou asked for this. The report is committed before the email, so it is always saved. The email is an extra.

See in code (1 block)

39

All 69 semicolons at the end of statements are removed

WhatThe first version kept ; on CREATE INDEX lines, after VALUES (...), after the DECLARE ... CASE ... END, after THROW, after BEGIN TRANSACTION / COMMIT TRANSACTION and after the email call. They are all removed. Nothing else in the code changed.

WhyCLAUDE.md rule 9 (new on main): do not add ; unless necessary. Microsoft's T-SQL parser reads the SP with and without them as the same 227 statements. The one place where SQL Server itself needs ; is before THROW when it directly follows another statement. In this SP both THROWs come after IF, so this rule does not apply. If SQL Server ever says "Incorrect syntax near THROW", put ; at the end of the line before the IF.

See in code (3 blocks)

40

_old.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M is in its own file

WhatThe old procedure is moved from DB_Objects.txt to _old.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M.sql. Its text is not changed, byte for byte. DB_Objects.txt now has only the new _report.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M.

WhyBoth procedures had the same change history at the top, so the file looked like one long SP and the _old INSERT looked like a part of the new SP.

41

#OutputBase: one thread and the full function name

WhatIn the US #OutputBase and the Canada #CA_OutputBase query, the call is HP_MAP_Prod.dbo.FN_GET_SELLER_STATUS again (as in Prod), and OPTION (MAXDOP 1) is added at the end of the query. One header line is added.

WhyThe first test run stopped after one hour with more than 100 GB of tempdb in the US #OutputBase step. With the short name SQL Server put the function code into the query and ran it on 8 threads, and the threads spilled their work to tempdb (199 GB of work space against 0.7 GB in the manual SP). The manual SP and Prod use the full name, run on one thread and take about 40 minutes. The rows are the same, only the plan changes.

42

No more join hint warning messages

WhatThe two INNER LOOP JOIN (US and Canada listing step in the daily loop) are now JOIN with OPTION (LOOP JOIN, FORCE ORDER, MAXDOP 2, RECOMPILE).

WhyYou asked for this. "The join order has been enforced because a local join hint is used" is printed for every local join hint, two times per day of the loop. The query hints give the same plan without the message. The warning "Null value is eliminated by an aggregate" is not changed.

43

A full refresh deletes the rows outside the report period

WhatWith @P_Generate_Full_Refresh = 1, a second DELETE in the same transaction removes the US and Canada rows with a date before the report start date or after the through date.

WhyYou asked for this. DELETE is used, not TRUNCATE, because business users who run the SP may not have the right to truncate the table.

Kept on purpose

These parts are not business rules, but I did not remove them.

  • The transaction around the delete and the insert. BEGIN TRANSACTION ... COMMIT TRANSACTION with SET XACT_ABORT ON stays. It is two lines, and it makes sure the report table is never left with the old rows deleted and the new rows missing.
  • READ COMMITTED. The old SP uses READ UNCOMMITTED and WITH (NOLOCK). I did not copy that. It changes which rows are read, so it is behaviour, not style.
  • Everything that is there for speed. The daily loop, OPTION (MAXDOP 2, RECOMPILE), the index hints with INNER LOOP JOIN, every index on the temp tables, the small #..._Signals table and the DROP TABLE of the work tables are not changed.
  • All business rules. Every CASE, join, filter, ORDER BY and column of the report is the same. The names of the temp tables and the variables are not changed.

Seen, but not changed

I found these points while reading the code. They are your decision, so the code is the same as before.

  • The long violation query is written two times for each country. It is there once to create the empty #MAP_L12M_US_Base table and once inside the daily loop. This could be shorter, but the loop is a speed design and I cannot test the speed here, so I did not touch it.
  • A few more columns in #ViolationRows are not read later. Has_Accepted, Has_Rejected, Market_ID and Violation_ID_Selector are stored in #ViolationRows but no later step reads them. They cost almost nothing, so I left them.
  • Termination Date from Drop_Date. #ActiveQPP keeps only partners with Status = 'Active', so Partner_Status = 'Dropped' can never be true. Not changed.

How it was checked

  • Done. A small script compared the original code and the new code word by word, block by block. The script ignores only style: spaces, line breaks, ;, AS, the N prefix, square brackets, the word INNER, INSERT / INSERT INTO and the HP_MAP_Prod. prefix. Result: the only differences are the changes in the list above. They are coloured in the code below.
  • Done. All semicolons at the end of statements were removed, then both versions were parsed. The parser reads the same 227 statements, of the same type and on the same line, with and without them. SQL Server's own rule for THROW is not tested by this parser. It does not apply here, because both THROWs follow IF (change 39).
  • Done. Brackets, CASE ... END and BEGIN ... END pairs are counted and they match. Every statement starts after a complete statement.
  • Done. The new SP, DB_Objects.txt and _old.SP_VIOLATIONS_L12M.sql were parsed with Microsoft's T-SQL parser (ScriptDom, SQL Server 2022 grammar). Result: 0 syntax errors. The parser checks only the syntax. It does not check that the tables, columns and functions exist.

Not done: the new SP was not compiled and not run on a real SQL Server. This session has no SQL Server with your databases. So object names, the speed and the result on real data are not checked.

Please compile it in a test database first. Then compare the rows of one run with the current SP before you use it in production.

#MAP_L12M_US_Base (empty table)USCode changedProgress message removedN prefix removed ×4Comment rewrittenStyleChanges: 14 18 19 22 35+ style 25–34

This query only creates the empty table. It is the same query as in the loop below. It is kept because the loop is a speed design.

Before · builder SP · lines 541–667
541 DECLARE @MAP_L12M_US_BatchStart DATE=@StartDate,@MAP_L12M_US_BatchEnd DATE=DATEADD(DAY,1,@StartDate);
542 RAISERROR(N'L12M stage starting #MAP_L12M_US_Base',10,1) WITH NOWAIT;
543 SELECT IDENTITY(BIGINT,1,1) AS MAP_RowToken,base.* INTO #MAP_L12M_US_Base
544 FROM (
545SELECT
546 v.Violation_ID,
547 v.Fact_ID AS [Fact ID],
548 v.Week_Start_Date AS [Week],
549 v.Collected_Date AS [Date],
550 dp.Product_Category_Reporting AS [Product Category],
551 CASE
552 WHEN dp.CSKU_Flag = 'C-SKU' THEN 'C-SKU'
553 WHEN dp.Government_SKU_Flag = 'Government SKU'
554 AND dp.Product_Category_Reporting = 'Supplies' THEN 'G-SKU'
555 ELSE dp.Product_Type_Indirect_MAP
556 END AS [Product Subcategory],
557 COALESCE
558 (
559 v.Product_Type_Enforcement,
560 CASE
561 WHEN dp.Product_Type_Indirect_MAP IN ('Ink', 'Toner') THEN 'Ink & Toner'
562 WHEN dp.Product_Type_Indirect_MAP LIKE 'Large Format%' THEN 'Large Format'
563 ELSE dp.Product_Type_Indirect_MAP
564 END
565 ) AS [Product Subcategory Enforcement],
566 v.SKU,
567 UPPER(NULLIF(nm.ASIN, 'N/A')) AS ASIN,
568 /* ViolationsMAP_NewFramework does not have Product_Description. Get the same Dim_Product row as the category fields. */
569 dp.Product_Description AS Product_Description,
570 v.Seller_Name_Unified AS Account,
571 v.Seller_Name AS [Seller Name],
572 v.New_Location_ID AS [Location ID],
573 v.Master_SID,
574 v.Seller_Penalty_Type AS [Seller Penalty Type],
575 v.Seller_Reporting_Group AS [Reporting Group],
576 v.Market_Unified AS Market,
577 CASE WHEN ISNULL(v.Login_Type_ID, 1) = 3 THEN N'Free log-in' ELSE 'Public Site' END AS [Log-in Type],
578 v.Price_Location AS [Price Location],
579 v.MAP,
580 v.Seller_Advertised_Price AS [Advertised Price],
581 v.Eligible_IR_Amount AS [Eligible IR],
582 v.Lowest_Possible_Price AS [Lowest Possible Price],
583 COALESCE(NULLIF(v.Screenshot_URL, 'N/A'), v.Product_Page, nm.Listing_URL, 'N/A') AS Screenshot_URL,
584 ISNULL(v.Validation_Status, 'Not Inspected') AS Validation_Status_Raw,
585 COALESCE(hpres.Reason_Description, res.Reason_Description, v.Comments) AS Notes,
586 v.Tier_Pass_Through,
587 ISNULL(v.Flag_Bundle, 0) AS Flag_Bundle,
588 ISNULL(v.Login_Type_ID, 1) AS Login_Type_ID,
589 CASE WHEN v.Violation_Code = 8 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS Flag_StrikeThrough,
590 CASE
591 WHEN v.Violation_Code = 6 THEN 'C-SKU - Restricted'
592 WHEN v.Violation_Code = 8 THEN 'Strike-through'
593 WHEN v.Violation_Code = 9 THEN N'Price - Coupon'
594 WHEN v.Flag_Bundle = 1 THEN 'Price - Bundle'
595 --WHEN v.Price_Location = 'Product Page Ad' THEN 'Price - Product Page'
596 WHEN v.Price_Location IN ('Product Page Ad', 'Product Page') THEN 'Price - Product Page'
597 WHEN v.Price_Location = 'Shopping Cart' THEN 'Price - In-Cart'
598 ELSE 'Electronic Content'
599 END AS Violation_Type,
600 CASE
601 WHEN v.Market IN ('Websites US', 'Best Buy') --CH 9/24/19 Added Best Buy
602 OR (v.Market = N'Amazon' AND v.New_Location_ID = '10322374')
603 OR (v.Market = 'Newegg' AND v.New_Location_ID = '10319396')
604 OR (v.Market = 'Walmart' AND v.New_Location_ID = '10256711') THEN 1
605 WHEN v.Market = N'Amazon' THEN 2
606 WHEN v.Market = 'Walmart' THEN 3
607 WHEN v.Market = 'eBay' THEN 4
608 WHEN v.Market = 'Newegg' THEN 5
609 ELSE 6
610 END AS Market_ID,
611 CASE
612 WHEN qpp.Partner_Count > 0
613 THEN HP_MAP_Prod.dbo.FN_GET_SELLER_STATUS
614 (
615 dp.Product_Category_Reporting,
616 sel.Supplies_Qualification,
617 sel.Print_Qualification,
618 sel.Qualified_Business_Personal_Systems,
619 sel.Qualified_Consumer_Personal_Systems
620 )
621 ELSE 'Unauthorized'
622 END AS Seller_Status,
623 CASE
624 WHEN qpp.Partner_Status = 'Dropped' THEN qpp.Drop_Date
625 WHEN qpp.Action_Required = N'PA Termination' THEN qpp.Expiration_Date
626 ELSE NULL
627 END AS [Termination Date],
628 v.Notification_ID,
629 linked_notification.DGF_Penalty,
630 CASE
631 WHEN v.Validation_Status = 'Accepted'
632 AND LEFT(linked_notification.Letter_Type, 1) = 'W' THEN 1
633 ELSE 0
634 END AS Flag_Triggered_Warning
635 FROM #MAP_L12M_US_Batch AS v
636 INNER JOIN #MAP_L12M_US_Product AS dp
637 ON dp.SKU = v.SKU
638 AND v.Collected_Date BETWEEN dp.Valid_From AND dp.Valid_To
639 AND dp.In_MAP_Scope_Flag = 'In MAP Scope'
640 INNER JOIN #MAP_L12M_US_Seller AS sel
641 ON sel.Master_New_Location_Id = v.New_Location_ID
642 AND v.Collected_Date BETWEEN sel.Valid_From AND sel.Valid_To
643 LEFT JOIN #MAP_L12M_US_Listings AS nm
644 ON nm.ID = v.Listing_ID
645 AND v.Data_Source IN ('NewMo', 'Pervasive Mind')
646 LEFT JOIN HP_MAP_Prod._ref.Rejection_Reasons AS res
647 ON res.Reason_ID = v.Reason_ID
648 LEFT JOIN HP_MAP_Prod._ref.HP_Rejection_Reasons AS hpres
649 ON hpres.HP_Reason_ID = v.HP_Reason_ID
650 LEFT JOIN #ActiveQPP AS qpp
651 ON qpp.HPI_Location_ID = v.New_Location_ID
652 OUTER APPLY
653 (
654 SELECT TOP (1)
655 n.Letter_Type,
656 n.DGF_Penalty
657 FROM #ValidNotifications AS n
658 WHERE n.Notification_ID = v.Notification_ID
659 AND n.New_Location_ID = v.New_Location_ID

661 ORDER BY n.Generation_Date DESC, n.ID DESC
662 ) AS linked_notification
663 WHERE v.Collected_Date >= @MAP_L12M_US_BatchStart
664 AND v.Collected_Date < @MAP_L12M_US_BatchEnd -- 4/6/20 LU: due to intraday launch, need to restrict violations from current date
665 AND (qpp.Partner_Count > 0 OR v.New_Location_ID = '99999999')
666 ) AS base WHERE 1=0;

506 OR (v.Market = 'Amazon' AND v.New_Location_ID = '10322374')
507 OR (v.Market = 'Newegg' AND v.New_Location_ID = '10319396')
508 OR (v.Market = 'Walmart' AND v.New_Location_ID = '10256711') THEN 1
509 WHEN v.Market = 'Amazon' THEN 2
510 WHEN v.Market = 'Walmart' THEN 3
511 WHEN v.Market = 'eBay' THEN 4
512 WHEN v.Market = 'Newegg' THEN 5
513 ELSE 6
514 END Market_ID
515 ,v.Notification_ID
516 ,linked_notification.DGF_Penalty
517 ,CASE
518 WHEN v.Validation_Status = 'Accepted' AND LEFT(linked_notification.Letter_Type, 1) = 'W' THEN 1
519 ELSE 0
520 END Flag_Triggered_Warning
521 FROM #MAP_L12M_US_Batch v
522 -- The product row must be valid on the violation date. This keeps old violations in the report when the SKU later goes out of scope.
523 JOIN #MAP_L12M_US_Product dp
524 ON dp.SKU = v.SKU
525 AND v.Collected_Date BETWEEN dp.Valid_From AND dp.Valid_To
526 AND dp.In_MAP_Scope_Flag = 'In MAP Scope'
527 JOIN #MAP_L12M_US_Seller sel
528 ON sel.Master_New_Location_Id = v.New_Location_ID
529 AND v.Collected_Date BETWEEN sel.Valid_From AND sel.Valid_To
530 -- Pervasive Mind rows use the same listing table as NewMo rows.
531 LEFT JOIN #MAP_L12M_US_Listings nm
532 ON nm.ID = v.Listing_ID
533 AND v.Data_Source IN ('NewMo', 'Pervasive Mind')
534 LEFT JOIN _ref.Rejection_Reasons res ON res.Reason_ID = v.Reason_ID
535 LEFT JOIN _ref.HP_Rejection_Reasons hpres ON hpres.HP_Reason_ID = v.HP_Reason_ID
536 LEFT JOIN #ActiveQPP qpp ON qpp.HPI_Location_ID = v.New_Location_ID
537 OUTER APPLY (
538 SELECT TOP (1) n.Letter_Type
539 ,n.DGF_Penalty
540 FROM #ValidNotifications n
541 WHERE n.Notification_ID = v.Notification_ID
542 AND n.New_Location_ID = v.New_Location_ID
543 AND n.Product_Type = v.Product_Type_Enforcement
544 ORDER BY n.Generation_Date DESC, n.ID DESC
545 ) linked_notification
546 WHERE v.Collected_Date >= @MAP_L12M_US_BatchStart
547 AND v.Collected_Date < @MAP_L12M_US_BatchEnd -- 4/6/20 LU: due to intraday launch, need to restrict violations from current date
548 AND (qpp.Partner_Count > 0 OR v.New_Location_ID = '99999999')
549 OPTION (MAXDOP 2, RECOMPILE)
550
551 SET @MAP_L12M_US_BatchStart = @MAP_L12M_US_BatchEnd
552 SET @MAP_L12M_US_BatchEnd = DATEADD(DAY, 1, @MAP_L12M_US_BatchStart)
553END

1032 w.[Date],
1033 p.[Product Category],
1034 p.[Product Subcategory],
1035 p.[Product Subcategory Enforcement],
1036 p.[Rule Product Type],
1037 s.Account,
1038 ISNULL(LEFT(sf.StoreFrontName, 4000), 'Seller not Marketplace Approved') AS [Storefront Name(s)], -- 3/20 LU added for Storefront Name(s)
1039 s.[Location ID],
1040 s.Master_SID,
1041 ISNULL(rg.Penalty_Type, N'30, 60, 90 Suspension') AS [Seller Penalty Type],
1042 ISNULL(rg.Reporting_Group, 'All') AS [Reporting Group] -- 3/6 LU Added for MPA Reporting Group
1043 INTO #CTE_cj

1046 CROSS JOIN #Sellers AS s
1047 LEFT JOIN #StoreFrontNames AS sf
1048 ON sf.New_Location_ID = s.[Location ID]

1053 r.Penalty_Type,
1054 r.Reporting_Group
1055 FROM [_ref].[Seller_Reporting_Group] AS r

1057 AND
1058 (

1060 OR r.Product_Type_Threshold = p.[Product Subcategory Enforcement]
1061 )
1062 AND w.[Date] BETWEEN r.Valid_From AND r.Valid_To
1063 ORDER BY
1064 CASE WHEN r.Master_SID = s.Master_SID THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
1065 CASE WHEN r.Product_Type = p.[Rule Product Type] THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
1066 r.Valid_From DESC,
1067 r.ID DESC

732INTO #CTE_cj
733FROM #Weeks w
734CROSS JOIN #Product_Category p
735CROSS JOIN #Sellers s
736LEFT JOIN #StoreFrontNames sf
737 ON sf.New_Location_ID = s.[Location ID]
738 AND sf.Country = 'US'
739OUTER APPLY (
740 SELECT TOP (1) r.Penalty_Type
741 ,r.Reporting_Group
742 FROM _ref.Seller_Reporting_Group r
743 WHERE r.Master_SID IN (s.Master_SID, -9999)
744 AND (r.Product_Type = p.[Rule Product Type] OR r.Product_Type_Threshold = p.[Product Subcategory Enforcement])
745 AND w.[Date] BETWEEN r.Valid_From AND r.Valid_To
746 ORDER BY CASE WHEN r.Master_SID = s.Master_SID THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
747 ,CASE WHEN r.Product_Type = p.[Rule Product Type] THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
748 ,r.Valid_From DESC
749 ,r.ID DESC
750) rg

1458 AND s.Penalty_Period_End_Date_Adjusted
1459 ORDER BY s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date DESC, s.ID DESC
1460 ) AS warning_period
1461 OUTER APPLY
1462 (
1463 SELECT TOP (1)
1464 s.ID,
1465 s.Violation_Status,
1466 s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date,
1467 s.Penalty_Period_End_Date_Adjusted

1472 AND scaffold.[Date] BETWEEN s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date
1473 AND s.Penalty_Period_End_Date_Adjusted
1474 ORDER BY s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date DESC, s.ID DESC
1475 ) AS consequence_period
1476 OUTER APPLY
1477 (
1478 SELECT
1479 CASE
1480 WHEN warning_period.ID IS NOT NULL THEN
1481 CASE warning_period.Violation_Status
1482 WHEN 'WF' THEN 'CF'
1483 WHEN 'W30' THEN 'C30'
1484 WHEN 'W60' THEN 'C60'
1485 WHEN 'W90' THEN 'C90'
1486 ELSE 'C30'
1487 END
1488 WHEN COALESCE(violation.[Seller Penalty Type], scaffold.[Seller Penalty Type]) LIKE '%Financial'
1489 THEN 'WF'
1490 WHEN consequence_period.Violation_Status = 'C30' THEN 'W60'
1491 WHEN consequence_period.Violation_Status IN ('C60', 'C90') THEN 'W90'
1492 ELSE 'W30'
1493 END AS Rule_Action
1494 ) AS threshold_action
1495 OUTER APPLY
1496 (
1497 SELECT TOP (1)
1498 r.Threshold_Action
1499 FROM [_ref].[Violation_Rules] AS r
1500 WHERE r.Program = 'MAP'
1501 AND r.Seller_Group IN
1502 (COALESCE(violation.[Reporting Group], scaffold.[Reporting Group], 'All'), 'All')
1503 AND r.Penalty_Description = COALESCE(violation.[Seller Penalty Type], scaffold.[Seller Penalty Type])
1504 AND r.Product_Type_Hybrid = scaffold.[Rule Product Type]
1505 AND r.[Action] = threshold_action.Rule_Action
1506 AND scaffold.[Date] BETWEEN r.Valid_From AND r.Valid_To
1507 ORDER BY
1508 CASE WHEN r.Seller_Group = COALESCE(violation.[Reporting Group], scaffold.[Reporting Group], 'All') THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
1509 r.Valid_From DESC,
1510 r.ID DESC
1511 ) AS threshold_rule
1512 OUTER APPLY
1513 (

1515 CASE
1516 WHEN warning_period.ID IS NULL THEN 1
1517 WHEN scaffold.[Product Subcategory] IN ('Ink', 'Toner', 'C-SKU', 'G-SKU', 'Large Format Supplies') THEN 5
1518 ELSE 2
1519 END AS Threshold_Action
1520 ) AS threshold_fallback
1521 LEFT JOIN #CTE_Contacts AS contacts
1522 ON contacts.[New Location ID] = scaffold.[Location ID] -- 9/10/20 LU added for G20 Contacts
1523 AND contacts.[Product Category] =

1526 WHEN scaffold.[Product Subcategory] IN ('C-SKU', 'G-SKU') THEN 'Supplies' --DP 1/12/23 adjusting join so Supplies contacts apply to C-SKU in tooltip
1527 ELSE scaffold.[Product Category]
1528 END
1529 LEFT JOIN #UPC_US AS upc
1530 ON upc.SKU = violation.SKU

1552 DROP TABLE IF EXISTS #WeeklySellerRanks;
1553 RAISERROR(N'L12M stage starting #WeeklySellerRanks',10,1) WITH NOWAIT;
1554 WITH SellerWeeks AS
1555 (
1556 SELECT
1557 output_base.Country,
1558 output_base.[Week],
1559 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), output_base.[Product Subcategory Enforcement]) AS [Product Subcategory Enforcement],
1560 output_base.Account,
1561 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(20), output_base.[Location ID]) AS [Location ID],
1562 MAX(output_base.Top_Reseller_Flag) AS Top_Reseller_Flag,
1563 COUNT_BIG(DISTINCT output_base.SKU) AS Violating_SKU_Count
1564 FROM #OutputBase AS output_base
1565 GROUP BY
1566 output_base.Country,
1567 output_base.[Week],
1568 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), output_base.[Product Subcategory Enforcement]),
1569 output_base.Account,
1570 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(20), output_base.[Location ID])
1571 )
1572 SELECT
1573 Country,
1574 [Week],
1575 [Product Subcategory Enforcement],
1576 [Location ID],
1577 DENSE_RANK() OVER
1578 (
1579 PARTITION BY Country, [Week], [Product Subcategory Enforcement]
1580 ORDER BY Top_Reseller_Flag DESC, Violating_SKU_Count DESC, Account
1581 ) AS Weekly_Seller_Rank
1582 INTO #WeeklySellerRanks
1583 FROM SellerWeeks;

1126-- Rank the sellers for each week and enforcement subcategory. Only rows that are in the report are counted.
1127-- The rank uses the number of different SKUs with violations in the week. This count is not used for any penalty calculation.
1128DROP TABLE IF EXISTS #WeeklySellerRanks
1129SELECT Country
1130 ,[Week]
1131 ,[Product Subcategory Enforcement]
1132 ,[Location ID]
1133 ,DENSE_RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY Country, [Week], [Product Subcategory Enforcement] ORDER BY Top_Reseller_Flag DESC, Violating_SKU_Count DESC, Account) Weekly_Seller_Rank
1134INTO #WeeklySellerRanks
1135FROM (
1136 SELECT output_base.Country
1137 ,output_base.[Week]
1138 ,CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), output_base.[Product Subcategory Enforcement]) [Product Subcategory Enforcement]
1139 ,output_base.Account
1140 ,CONVERT(NVARCHAR(20), output_base.[Location ID]) [Location ID]
1141 ,MAX(output_base.Top_Reseller_Flag) Top_Reseller_Flag
1142 ,COUNT(DISTINCT output_base.SKU) Violating_SKU_Count
1143 FROM #OutputBase output_base
1144 GROUP BY output_base.Country
1145 ,output_base.[Week]
1146 ,CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), output_base.[Product Subcategory Enforcement])
1147 ,output_base.Account
1148 ,CONVERT(NVARCHAR(20), output_base.[Location ID])
1149) SellerWeeks

1905 Fiscal_Week_Number

2035 ELSE 0

2056 LEFT JOIN #CA_ActiveQPP AS qpp

2071 AND (qpp.Partner_Count > 0 OR v.New_Location_ID = N'99999999')

2820 LEFT JOIN #CA_ViolationRows AS violation
2821 ON violation.[Location ID] = scaffold.[Location ID]
2822 AND violation.Master_SID = scaffold.Master_SID --rv 4/12/23
2823 AND violation.[Product Subcategory] = scaffold.[Product Subcategory]
2824 AND violation.[Date] = scaffold.[Date]
2825 LEFT JOIN #CA_Fiscal_Week AS fiscal
2826 ON fiscal.Week_Begin_Date_Alt = scaffold.[Week]
2827 LEFT JOIN #CA_TopResellers AS top_seller
2828 ON top_seller.New_Location_ID = scaffold.[Location ID]
2829 OUTER APPLY
2830 (
2831 SELECT TOP (1)
2832 ds.Supplies_Qualification,
2833 ds.Print_Qualification,
2834 ds.Qualified_Business_Personal_Systems,
2835 ds.Qualified_Consumer_Personal_Systems
2836 FROM HP_MAP_Canada._final.Dim_Seller AS ds
2837 WHERE ds.Master_SID = scaffold.Master_SID
2838 AND scaffold.[Date] BETWEEN ds.Valid_From AND ds.Valid_To
2839 ORDER BY ds.Valid_From DESC, ds.Seller_ID DESC
2840 ) AS seller_at_date
2841 LEFT JOIN #CA_ActiveQPP AS qpp
2842 ON qpp.HPI_Location_ID = scaffold.[Location ID]
2843 LEFT JOIN #CA_LatestNotifications AS latest_notification
2844 ON latest_notification.New_Location_ID = scaffold.[Location ID]
2845 AND latest_notification.Product_Type = scaffold.[Product Subcategory Enforcement]
2846 LEFT JOIN #CA_LatestStrikes AS latest_strike
2847 ON latest_strike.New_Location_ID = scaffold.[Location ID]
2848 AND latest_strike.Product_Type = scaffold.[Product Subcategory Enforcement]
2849 /* Historical-period approach: inactive/rehabilitated strikes remain valid
2850 evidence that an older report date was inside a Warning monitoring period. */
2851 OUTER APPLY
2852 (
2853 SELECT TOP (1)
2854 s.ID,
2855 s.Violation_Status,
2856 s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date,
2857 s.Penalty_Period_End_Date_Adjusted
2858 FROM #CA_ValidStrikes AS s
2859 WHERE s.New_Location_ID = scaffold.[Location ID]
2860 AND s.Product_Type = scaffold.[Product Subcategory Enforcement]
2861 AND LEFT(s.Violation_Status, 1) = N'W'
2862 AND scaffold.[Date] BETWEEN s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date
2863 AND s.Penalty_Period_End_Date_Adjusted
2864 ORDER BY s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date DESC, s.ID DESC
2865 ) AS warning_period
2866 OUTER APPLY
2867 (
2868 SELECT TOP (1)
2869 s.ID,
2870 s.Violation_Status,
2871 s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date,
2872 s.Penalty_Period_End_Date_Adjusted
2873 FROM #CA_ValidStrikes AS s
2874 WHERE s.New_Location_ID = scaffold.[Location ID]
2875 AND s.Product_Type = scaffold.[Product Subcategory Enforcement]
2876 AND LEFT(s.Violation_Status, 1) = N'C'
2877 AND scaffold.[Date] BETWEEN s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date
2878 AND s.Penalty_Period_End_Date_Adjusted
2879 ORDER BY s.Penalty_Period_Start_Date DESC, s.ID DESC
2880 ) AS consequence_period
2881 OUTER APPLY
2882 (
2883 SELECT
2884 CASE
2885 WHEN warning_period.ID IS NOT NULL THEN
2886 CASE warning_period.Violation_Status
2887 WHEN N'WF' THEN N'CF'
2888 WHEN N'W30' THEN N'C30'
2889 WHEN N'W60' THEN N'C60'
2890 WHEN N'W90' THEN N'C90'
2891 ELSE N'C30'
2892 END
2893 WHEN COALESCE(violation.[Seller Penalty Type], scaffold.[Seller Penalty Type]) LIKE N'%Financial'
2894 THEN N'WF'
2895 WHEN consequence_period.Violation_Status = N'C30' THEN N'W60'
2896 WHEN consequence_period.Violation_Status IN (N'C60', N'C90') THEN N'W90'
2897 ELSE N'W30'
2898 END AS Rule_Action
2899 ) AS threshold_action
2900 OUTER APPLY
2901 (
2902 SELECT TOP (1)
2903 r.Threshold_Action
2904 FROM [HP_MAP_Canada].[_ref].[Violation_Rules] AS r
2905 WHERE r.Program = N'MAP'
2906 AND r.Seller_Group IN
2907 (COALESCE(violation.[Reporting Group], scaffold.[Reporting Group], N'All'), N'All')
2908 AND r.Penalty_Description = COALESCE(violation.[Seller Penalty Type], scaffold.[Seller Penalty Type])
2909 AND r.Product_Type_Hybrid = scaffold.[Rule Product Type]
2910 AND r.[Action] = threshold_action.Rule_Action
2911 AND scaffold.[Date] BETWEEN r.Valid_From AND r.Valid_To
2912 ORDER BY
2913 CASE WHEN r.Seller_Group = COALESCE(violation.[Reporting Group], scaffold.[Reporting Group], N'All') THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
2914 r.Valid_From DESC,
2915 r.ID DESC
2916 ) AS threshold_rule
2917 OUTER APPLY
2918 (
2919 SELECT
2920 CASE
2921 WHEN warning_period.ID IS NULL THEN 1
2922 WHEN scaffold.[Product Subcategory] IN (N'Ink', N'Toner', N'C-SKU', N'G-SKU')
2923 THEN CASE WHEN COALESCE(violation.[Seller Penalty Type],scaffold.[Seller Penalty Type]) LIKE N'%Financial%' THEN 10 ELSE 5 END
2924 ELSE 2
2925 END AS Threshold_Action
2926 ) AS threshold_fallback
2927 LEFT JOIN #CA_CTE_Contacts AS contacts
2928 ON contacts.[New Location ID] = scaffold.[Location ID] -- 9/10/20 LU added for G20 Contacts
2929 AND contacts.[Product Category] =
2930 CASE
2931 WHEN scaffold.[Product Subcategory] LIKE N'Large Format%' THEN N'Large Format'
2932 WHEN scaffold.[Product Subcategory] IN (N'C-SKU', N'G-SKU') THEN N'Supplies' --DP 1/12/23 adjusting join so Supplies contacts apply to C-SKU in tooltip
2933 ELSE scaffold.[Product Category]
2934 END
2935 LEFT JOIN #CA_UPC_US AS upc
2936 ON upc.SKU = violation.SKU
2937 LEFT JOIN #CA_MinViolationDate AS min_violation
2938 ON min_violation.New_Location_ID = scaffold.[Location ID]
2939 LEFT JOIN #CA_Min_Max_Listing_Date AS listing_dates
2940 ON listing_dates.Master_New_Location_Id = scaffold.[Location ID]
2941 LEFT JOIN #CA_DateRequested AS date_requested
2942 ON date_requested.Master_New_Location_Id = scaffold.[Location ID]
2943 AND date_requested.Ctry = N'Canada'
2944 LEFT JOIN #CA_NewSKU AS new_sku
2945 ON new_sku.SKU = violation.SKU
2946 LEFT JOIN #CA_Content AS content
2947 ON content.HPI_Location_ID = scaffold.[Location ID]
2948 AND content.Country IN (N'CA', N'Canada')
2949 LEFT JOIN #CA_PartnerLookup AS partner
2950 ON partner.LocationId = scaffold.[Location ID]
2951 WHERE scaffold.[Location ID] <> N'N/A'; -- 10/14/24 ED

main SP · lines 121–146
121 /* MAP_L12M_MAIL_AFTER_COMMIT_V1: queue only after successful publication.
122 Mail failure cannot roll back an already committed report or invite an
123 automatic retry of the expensive refresh. Queued does not mean delivered. */
124 BEGIN TRY
125 IF HP_MAP_Prod.dbo.CanSendDbMail() = 1
126 BEGIN
127 DECLARE @L12M_MailReturn INT, @L12M_MailItemID INT;
128 EXEC @L12M_MailReturn = msdb.dbo.sp_send_dbmail
129 @profile_name = N'QDN Support',
130 @recipients = N'pbelezhkov@dsmengines.com;edeneva@dsmengines.com;zfraile@dsmengines.com',
131 @body = N'The _report.Violations_L12M table has been refreshed.',
132 @subject = N'The Violations L12M export has been generated',
133 @body_format = N'HTML',
134 @mailitem_id = @L12M_MailItemID OUTPUT;
135 IF ISNULL(@L12M_MailReturn, -1) <> 0
136 THROW 51000, N'Database Mail did not return success.', 1;
137 RAISERROR(N'L12M publication COMMITTED; success email queued (mailitem_id %d).', 10, 1, @L12M_MailItemID) WITH NOWAIT;
138 END;
139 ELSE
140 RAISERROR(N'L12M publication COMMITTED; email disabled by CanSendDbMail().', 10, 1) WITH NOWAIT;
141 END TRY
142 BEGIN CATCH
143 DECLARE @L12M_MailError NVARCHAR(2048) = ERROR_MESSAGE();
144 RAISERROR(N'L12M publication COMMITTED, but its success email was not queued: %s. Investigate mail; do not rerun the refresh only to resend email.', 10, 1, @L12M_MailError) WITH NOWAIT;
145 END CATCH;
146END;
